It is a gentler way of seeing change: every small action is helping me answer the question of who I believe I am.","My paraphrase of James Clear's idea: tiny habits do more than change what I do; they give me evidence about the person I believe I am becoming.","Three levels of change"],"opening":true},{"kind":"content","paragraphs":["The book helped me see change through three levels: outcomes are \"what I get,\" processes are \"what I do each day,\" and identity is \"who I want to become.\"","<li><strong>Outcomes</strong> -> What I get.</li> <li><strong>Processes</strong> -> What I do each day.</li> <li><strong>Identity</strong> -> Who I want to become.</li>","For me, the most important point sits at the identity level.
